Root cause (in-process t1mix import with `--cpu-prof` and an event-loop probe, `node:perf_hooks`): 45 of 48 s CPU in synchronous SQLite commits; the parse-commit loop over one 1 MiB read chunk was a microtask chain, so a 5 ms timer ran p95 97 / p99 200 / max 707 ms late; Codex and agy watchers did not exist during the Claude backfill (adapters started one after another); settle was detected on the 250 ms tick. Variants measured (yield only; 25 or 50 or 10 rows; 128 KiB or 256 KiB or 64 KiB; read chunk 128 KiB; with or without an immediate flush; Node "Don't block the event loop" guidance): chosen 25 rows / 128 KiB / 128 KiB chunks + immediate flush + `setImmediate` after each commit (p95 ~20 / p99 ~27 / max ~73 ms, import ~51 s); 25 rows without the immediate flush took ~451 s (the 50 ms coalescing timer per batch). Change: `tail-adapter.ts` (defaults, flush every full batch, yield, `watch()`/`backfill()`), `boot.ts` (every watcher before any backfill; `onTick` with one timer at the earliest watched `settleAt`, incremental per watch, rescan on the tick and the timer), `status/engine.ts` (`settleAt`), `index.ts` (pushes `onTick` keys). RED 174 (4 failures for their intended reasons) → GREEN 176 (863/863; run 175 failed only the oversized-batch test, which did not reach the oversized path; corrected to force it and bound FTS content per batch, checked failing at the old 1 MiB settings: 600 KiB vs 196 KiB). Review (ecc:code-reviewer, sonnet, fresh context, diff-only): approve-with-fixes, no critical/high; M1 (a full rescan per watch: 5,000 rows ~1.8–3.9 s) fixed RED-first (RED 177 → GREEN 178, 864/864); M2 (0 ms timer loop with a frozen clock) not possible (Finishing… holds only while clock < settleAt, so the delay is ≥ 2 ms); L3 (re-emitted rows after a crash mid oversized line) covered by the existing restart tests (kill/restart mid-backfill, EC-15 restart-safe: no duplicates); L4 settle test bound loosened to 200 ms. `test:electron` 355/355; E2E 20/20 on the rebuilt package; typecheck, lint, `privacy:scan` pass. Packaged re-measurement (Docker stopped): see §7 M1, M2, M3 (all pass). Probe fixes (test only): the import probe's clone keeps timestamps; spooled hooks count as misses in its M1 figure. **Codex review** (`codex exec -s read-only`, diff-only; owner request; two plugin runs stalled before reporting and a first direct run waited on stdin): approve-with-fixes, no critical/high; M1 (a record below the 8 MiB parse cap still commits as one record) measured, accepted as a limit: one valid line holds the loop ~7 ms at 1 MiB, ~24 ms at 3 MiB, ~43 ms at 6 MiB (~142 ms under the parallel suite); lowering the cap would move 1–8 MiB lines to the oversized path, which keeps only small fields (usage and tool rows lost); a hook arriving during such a commit may spool and is replayed; upgrade path: a writer worker thread if real stores show such lines during live work; guarded by a load-tolerant regression test (< 400 ms); L2 (the settle test could lose its window to a slow boot) fixed: a status clock frozen through boot, plus tests for an earlier deadline moving the timer and for no tick after `stop()` with a timer pending. Design security review (ecc:security-reviewer, opus, fresh context) approve-with-fixes before code; adopted H1 (intake refuses a suppressed session before publish or chunk write), H2 (backups via VACUUM INTO), H3 (pending file work recorded in the marker, redone at boot), M1, M4, M5, L1, L2. Done: migration 022 `suppressions` (identity, reason manual/retention, at, pending registration ids); `src/service/history/purge.ts` `purgeSession(writer, id, {dataDir, backupDir, secret, now, reason})`: one writer transaction settles (FR-52), removes FTS rows with their indexed prefix, deletes the session's rows (events, turns, tool calls, usage, cost, context/usage snapshots, session-linked quota scopes and history, cwd history, subagents, links, quarantine of its files, report content/deliveries, hook deliveries/gaps, status evidence incl. legacy profile '', overlays), keeps `source_files` rows with session_id NULL (the native file name is a rescan checkpoint, not Xaibo content), writes the marker; after COMMIT only: removes chunk items (`removeItems`), rewrites closed spool files (past UTC dates, taken replay files; quiet 5 s on mtime/ctime; inode/size re-check), deletes backups holding the session and any partial backup (Q-5.1: `backupsDeleted` is the notice), clears pending, incremental_vacuum best effort; `resumePurges` (boot, off readiness). Writer: suppressed sessions dropped from every batch and from onCommit, a suppressed delivery/report commits nothing, `transact` with the disk-full state, a throwing ROLLBACK keeps the original error and fences the connection until restart. | P4-T10b (status-engine session cache: load on demand, bounded LRU) | done (run 100) | XAIBO-PROD-425, new-feature run 100, risk high (keyword "session"; security review required). `src/service/status/engine.ts` no longer folds every session's `status_evidence` at start (the hook `seq` counter comes from `max(seq) WHERE rank = 0`): a session loads from the store on first `status` or `ingest` into a use-ordered LRU of at most `maxSessions` (default `MAX_SESSIONS` 500, ponytail), trimmed after each public call. Status is a pure function of the stored evidence, so a reload refolds to the same status, dedup and producer tops, and never notifies. A session is never evicted while it holds unstored evidence (a failed insert, or one ignored by the store's unique key, which spans profiles) or while its last read was busy or heuristic (boot reads those every tick). `cached()` added to `StatusEngine` for P4-T10c. Deviation: the plan says ended sessions; the LRU also bounds sessions that never end (silence never ends a session) and keeps recently read ended ones, so a list does not reload them per read. no migration; chunk rows are not counted in `record_count` (EC-04 ratio); (3) chunk key = hash of the line offset + first 4 KiB, so ids are the same for any input chunking and after a crash reparse; a record shifted by a file rewrite duplicates chunks rather than losing them; (4) readable paths: `message.content` (string), `message.content[].text|thinking|content|input.*`, `message.content[].content[].text`, top-level `content`, and S9's `attachment.content`, `rendered[].content`, `shape.system[].text`; small fields kept up to 4 KiB; a syntax error is an `oversized-unreadable` quarantine row plus file status `oversized`, content read before it stays; (5) chunk `source_seq` is exact only to one input piece (ponytail note); | Gate 3 (Claude, from an EC-46 failure under load): the forwarder arms its 60 ms `finish` deadline at module start, but the spool fallback (`unsettled`) is armed only after stdin is read; if startup stalls past 60 ms (a loaded machine), `finish` exits 0 with nothing spooled and no gap, so that hook is lost silently. Pre-existing since P3-T03 (a review comment already names an "EC-46 flake"); Codex did not flag it. Options: on the deadline, spool whatever stdin holds as a truncated envelope (evidence of a missed hook), or start the deadline when stdin is read (costs M16 budget under load), or accept as rare. ## 9a. Plan challenge record (Codex, 2026-11-09, plan v0.1 → v0.2)

Baseline `master` at `e6b39ab`; diff `master...feat/phase0-spikes`. Lens verdicts: traceability "yes with fixes"; sequencing "no"; safety/privacy "no"; verification/consistency "no".

| # | Sev | Finding (short) | Disposition in v0.2 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | blocker | Gate suite tests `HEAD` while review covers uncommitted changes; no full re-run after fixes; Gate 0/D review only committed diffs | Fixed: one gate SHA for suite, both reviews and owner approval; full re-run on each new SHA (§2.4, P0-G-T4, PD-T03, OQ-X7). *Historical: v0.2 also allowed a snapshot commit; removed by D-P0-07, current procedure per round 3 below.* |
| 2 | blocker | Runner refuses every path under the real home, but profiles live in the repo under home | Fixed: refuse overlap with real agent/Xaibo roots and anything outside `$P`; symlink and repo-under-home tests (P0-T05) |
| 3 | blocker | Guard lists real session stores; owner audit reads real stores | Fixed per D-P0-04: guard has no store listing; audit is the named owner-run exception (P0-T04, P0-S9-T3, AGENTS.md) |

| 5 | major | Guard misses deletions/removals; "volatile" hides changes; post-run only | Fixed: add/modify/delete/replace/truncate and keychain removal detected and tested; stated as detection only, prevention by runner + sandbox (P0-T04) |
| 6 | major | "Zero-write probe" not proven read-only; keychain checked after login | Fixed: credential backend proven isolated from docs before any probe; probe guarded; else UNVERIFIED (P0-T06) |
| 7 | major | Phase 0 privacy check misses ports, non-UUID IDs, most secrets; not run on the whole diff | Fixed: full D-20 pattern set from Phase 0, canary tests, whole gate diff via `--stdin`, manual review of reduced evidence (P0-T08) |
| 8 | major | Guard prints names; cleanup kills by `pgrep -f` substring | Fixed: category counts only; cleanup by recorded PID + start time (P0-T04, P0-T05, P0-T09) |
| 9 | major | P0-T03 packages the S3 SEA binary before P0-S3-T5 builds it | Fixed: entry added in P0-S3-T5 |
| 10 | major | electron-builder Phase 0 vs PRD App. C Phase 1 | Fixed per D-P0-05: PRD errata |
| 11 | major | TSX units have no harness-observable RED | Fixed: node:test RED on each unit's `.ts` logic before any `.tsx`; proved on the first Phase 1 `.tsx` unit (§3.6, P4-T03, OQ-X8) |
| 12 | major | Gate D contrast target undefined | Fixed: pair inventory + OQ-X15 thresholds (WCAG 2.2 AA default), per-pair report (PD-T01/T03, Gate D checklist) |
| 13 | major | EC-15 oversized record may stay unreadable (FR-08) | Fixed: bounded streaming extraction of readable content; gap only for unreadable bytes (P1-T14, OQ-P1-06, R-P1-09) |

**Fix-delta re-review 1 (Codex).** *Historical: the R1 and R2 mechanics below (snapshot commit, record in progress file) were superseded by D-P0-07 and rounds 2–3.* Of the 19: 12 resolved, 7 partially resolved (#1, #2, #5, #6, #15, #16, #17). Verdict "no". New findings and dispositions:

| # | Sev | Finding (short) | Disposition |
|---|---|---|---|
| R1 | blocker | Snapshot command can leak `GIT_INDEX_FILE` on failure; ignored tracked files can drop out | Fixed: subshell with `set -eu`, trap cleanup, index under `.git/`, `read-tree HEAD` seed, before/after check of branch and real index (§2.4 step 1) |
| R2 | blocker | Gate SHA recorded inside files that are part of that SHA; verdicts written after freeze | Fixed: gate SHA only in `.handoff/` during the gate; verdicts, dispositions and approval go in a separate gate-record commit checked by `git diff --stat` (§2.4 steps 1 and 8, P0-G-T4, PD-T05) |
| R3 | major | Gate D revisions do not re-run the design checks | Fixed: full PD-T03 checklist incl. per-pair contrast re-run on each new SHA (PD-T04) |
| R4 | major | Runner treats `GEMINI_CLI_HOME` as a root (it is the store's parent) | Fixed: real root is `${GEMINI_CLI_HOME}/.gemini`; tests with a real-home override and a custom parent (P0-T04, P0-T05) |
| R5 | major | Guard `MATCH` vs an expected new temp keychain item contradict | Fixed: `--expect-new-keychain <svce>` for the one pre-identified item; tests (P0-T04, P0-T06) |
| R6 | major | `--version` runs before the credential-backend check | Fixed: backend check is docs-only and comes first; first `--version` per agent is guarded and must `MATCH`; entry criterion 5 no longer runs agents in the real env (§0.2, P0-T05, P0-T06) |
| R7 | minor | P0-T01 status check ignores the earlier P0-T10 report | Fixed: before/after comparison (P0-T01) |
| R8 | major | No enforced write-deny for owner-run commands outside the sandbox | Fixed: runner wraps unsandboxed commands in a generated `sandbox-exec` deny profile with a canary self-check, refuses otherwise; keychain writes covered by guard + backend check (P0-T04, P0-T05) |
| R9 | minor | Gemini override experiment conflicts with the runner's fixed env | Fixed: runner `--gemini-layout unset\|custom` mode, tested (P0-T05, P0-S11-T1) |

**Fix-delta re-review 2 (Codex).** R3–R7 and R9 resolved; R1, R2, R8 partially; of the earlier partials #2, #5, #6, #17 resolved, #1, #15, #16 partially. Verdict "no". New findings and dispositions:

| # | Sev | Finding (short) | Disposition |
|---|---|---|---|

Diff `master` → working tree. 43 of 48 findings confirmed by a skeptic pass; duplicates merged below. Refuted (5): repo-wide tools reading in-repo temp credentials (graphify honours `.gitignore`); missing owner touchpoints (each task names its owner action); progress showing gate not passed after merge (by design, D-P0-07); P0-G-T5 "commit" wording (consistent with step 6); a "dead" `/logout` rule (used at retirement).

| X1 | major | Supervisor startup/shutdown races; group ID reuse; grandchildren cannot be reaped | Requirement stated; handshake over a pipe, TERM-ignoring sentinel keeps the group ID alive until signalling ends, fail closed on setup errors; waits for disappearance, does not reap; five race tests (P0-T05) |
| X2 | major | Keychain outcomes for mixed changes inconsistent across rules, tests and P0-T06 | Precedence defined (DIFF > unmatched-change > MATCH/expected-new); tests for expected+agent and expected+unrecognised; P0-T06 step 5 aligned (P0-T04, P0-T06) |

| # | Sev | Finding (short) | Disposition |
|---|---|---|---|

**Codex re-review of Y1–Y3.** Y2, Y3 resolved; Y1 partially. Instead of patching the supervisor again, it was simplified to standard job control:

|---|---|---|---|

| Z2 | major | Group ID could be reused between acknowledgement and sentinel start | Sentinel and handshake removed: parent and child both `setpgid`; the leader is not reaped until group signalling ends, so its ID stays reserved (P0-T05) |
| Z3 | minor | "No process remains" contradicted the escaped-survivor case | Expected states before and after test cleanup stated |

**Focused Codex check of Z1–Z3.** Z1, Z3 resolved; Z2 partially. **Codex verdict: "yes with fixes"** for owner approval. The one fix (applied): the parent's `setpgid` returning `EACCES` after the child has exec'd is the expected macOS race; the parent verifies the child's group ID and continues; genuine setup failures stop the group before reaping; test added (P0-T05).

## Update rule

- Update in the same working change as each task, as soon as it becomes done or blocked; on every design decision where the PRD left a detail open; on every owner decision; and before each gate review (the review diff includes this file). Never batch updates at phase end.
- Spike findings only in `phase0_spikes.md`; this file links to them.
- Repo privacy rules apply in full: no counts, paths, IDs, ports or prompts from the owner's machine.
